        # Implementation Plan: Implement ArtAgents Thread / Variant / Iteration-Video Layer

## Overview
Ship the 10-day sprint in `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`: Week 1 creates the durable thread/run/variant metadata layer at executor and orchestrator chokepoints, and Week 2 consumes that metadata to produce `builtin.iteration_video` outputs.

The new critique does not change the root cause of the overall plan, but it does identify one wrong implementation assumption in the iteration-video phase: `builtin.render` is not a generic renderer for arbitrary `iteration.timeline.json` names. It currently consumes `{brief_out}/hype.timeline.json` and `{brief_out}/hype.assets.json` and emits `{brief_out}/hype.mp4`. The plan must therefore make `iteration.assemble` a render-adapter stage that writes both human-facing iteration artifacts and render-compatible `hype.*` adapter files before invoking `builtin.render`.

The corrected iteration-video approach is: `iteration.prepare -> iteration.assemble -> builtin.render -> finalize/copy outputs`. `iteration.assemble` emits canonical `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.quality.json`, and `iteration.report.html`, and also materializes byte-equivalent render inputs at `{brief_out}/hype.timeline.json` and `{brief_out}/hype.assets.json`. The orchestrator then calls existing `builtin.render` against those `hype.*` names and copies or renames `{brief_out}/hype.mp4` to `iteration.mp4`. `builtin.cut` is not run after assemble because assemble has already produced the render-ready `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json` pair; this follows the repo rule that render can proceed without cut when both files already exist.

The implementation should bridge into existing systems instead of inventing parallel ones: use `artagents/audit/*` and hype audit parent propagation as provenance inputs, add the new thread block at allowed wrapper/orchestrator integration points rather than by modifying forbidden executor `run.py` files, keep generated dogfood media out of CI by using a tracked compact fixture, and preserve all DEF trims.

## Phase 1: Foundation — CLI Audit, Dependencies, IDs, Schema, Index

### Step 1: Audit current routing, provenance, and render surfaces (`artagents/pipeline.py`, `artagents/audit/`, `artagents/orchestrators/hype/run.py`, `artagents/executors/render/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Inspect** top-level command dispatch in `artagents/pipeline.py` and confirm where new `thread` and `modalities` commands must be registered for `python3 -m artagents`.
2. **Inspect** executor/orchestrator CLI construction to identify `out=""`, `out=None`, dry-run, command-runtime, and nested subprocess call paths before adding wrapper behavior.
3. **Inspect** `artagents/audit/*` and hype parent propagation via `ARTAGENTS_AUDIT_PARENT_IDS`; treat this as an existing provenance source to bridge into thread records and iteration-video ancestry.
4. **Inspect** `builtin.render` inputs and hype render command construction; record the compatibility contract that render consumes `{brief_out}/hype.timeline.json` plus `{brief_out}/hype.assets.json` and emits `{brief_out}/hype.mp4`.
5. **Run** cheap baseline checks: `python3 -m artagents --help`, `python3 -m artagents doctor`, `python3 -m artagents executors list`, `python3 -m artagents orchestrators list`, and `python3 -m artagents elements list`.

### Step 2: Add deterministic dependency plumbing (`requirements.txt`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `xxhash>=3.4` unconditionally to `requirements.txt` because SD-032 requires xxhash.
2. **Add** a dependency test that imports `xxhash` and confirms the requirement is declared.

### Step 3: Add thread state package (`artagents/threads/ids.py`, `artagents/threads/schema.py`, `artagents/threads/index.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Implement** monotonic 26-character Crockford ULIDs for `run_id`, `thread_id`, and `group_id`.
2. **Define** v1 schema constants and validators for `.artagents/threads.json`, `run.json`, `selections.jsonl`, `groups.json`, and hype provenance blocks.
3. **Implement** `.artagents/threads.json` with a sidecar lock file, `fcntl.flock`, 30-second timeout, tmp write, fsync, directory fsync, `os.replace`, and `.bak` rotation.
4. **Resolve** the sprint conflict around stale locks by not implementing `thread gc`; timeout errors should name supported manual remediation and `thread show/list` diagnostics, because SD-036 cuts `thread gc`.
5. **Reject** absolute persisted paths at schema/write boundaries; allow only repo-relative paths or content-addressed references.

### Step 4: Prove foundation behavior (`tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Test** schema creation, ULID format, path validation, lock acquisition, lock timeout text, atomic replacement, `.bak` recovery, and eight-writer concurrency.

3. **Test** `xxhash` importability and `requirements.txt` declaration.

## Phase 2: Runner Chokepoints and Failure-Safe Records

### Step 5: Build run record helpers (`artagents/threads/record.py`, `artagents/threads/wrapper.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Implement** `threads.begin(request)` and `threads.finalize(record_id, result_or_error)` around a normalized request shape shared by executors and orchestrators.
2. **Handle** missing/empty output paths before repo-relative/private-path logic. `out=None` or `out=""` should either no-op when appropriate or record a safe empty/unknown output state without crashing.
3. **Capture** the trimmed SD-008 field set, typed `parent_run_ids`, xxhash `inputs_digest`, sha256 output artifact hashes, `brief_content_sha256`, redacted CLI args, private-path sha-only metadata, and trimmed external model provenance.
4. **Bridge** existing audit provenance into thread records: map audit parent IDs/assets into typed causal `parent_run_ids` and input/output artifact provenance where available, then fall back to artifact hash matching.
5. **Do not** write trimmed fields: `host_id`, top-level `chosen_from_groups`, `preview_modes`, external seed/cost/latency, or low/high cost ranges.

### Step 6: Wrap executor and orchestrator runners with exception finalization (`artagents/executors/runner.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Extend** `ExecutorRunRequest` and `OrchestratorRunRequest` with `thread`, `variants`, and `from_variant`/`from` semantics.
2. **Wrap** `run_executor()` and `run_orchestrator()` with `try/except/finally` so any exception after `begin` writes `ended_at`, failure status, returncode/error metadata, and then re-raises the original exception.
3. **Preserve** no-op gates: dry run, missing/empty unwritable output where no run artifact can be safely recorded, temp output, `--thread @none`, `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1`, inherited child context, and `upload.youtube` zero-artifact behavior.
4. **Pass** `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID` and thread context into `_run_external_executor`, command-runtime orchestrators, and hype pipeline subprocesses launched outside `_run_external_executor`.
5. **Avoid** duplicate child begin records when a nested subprocess inherits `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID`; nested work should attach to parent context.

### Step 7: Wire run flags and top-level routes (`artagents/executors/cli.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `--thread <id|@new|@none>`, `--variants N`, and `--from <run-id>:<n>` to executor and orchestrator run commands.
2. **Register** new `thread` and `modalities` routes in `artagents/pipeline.py` so `python3 -m artagents thread ...` and `python3 -m artagents modalities ...` work through the package gateway.
3. **Keep** CLI parsing thin; centralize attribution, validation, and no-op decisions in `artagents/threads`.

**Scope:** Large

2. **Verify** exceptions after begin finalize records before re-raising.
3. **Verify** missing/empty `out` paths do not crash private-path or repo-relative validation.
4. **Verify** all no-op gates create no thread artifacts and no errors.
5. **Verify** nested command-runtime, hype subprocess, and external executor inheritance via `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID`.
6. **Verify** `upload.youtube` bypass behavior and redaction/private-path behavior.

### Step 9: Implement attribution and prefix contracts (`artagents/threads/attribute.py`, `artagents/threads/prefix.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Implement** SD-006 attribution under the index lock: explicit thread, lineage inference from `runs/<R>/` input-like args, active open thread, archived active thread within reopen window, then new thread.
2. **Implement** lazy lifecycle windows: `IDLE_ARCHIVE_WINDOW=7d`, `IDLE_REOPEN_WINDOW=48h`, open to archived to reopen, no daemon.
3. **Implement** SD-018 stdout prefix generation: `[thread]`, `[variants]` when present, optional `Notice:`, blank line, then command output.

3. **Do not add** `thread gc`, `split`, `merge`, `attach`, or `detach`.
4. **Implement** `thread backfill` for existing `runs/*/` directories without moving files, clustered by hash graph rather than mtime.

## Phase 4: Variants, Locked Group Indexes, and Provenance

### Step 13: Implement variant state (`artagents/threads/selections.py`, `artagents/threads/groups.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** append-only `.artagents/threads/<thread-id>/selections.jsonl` events with last-write-wins read semantics.
2. **Use** atomic append for `selections.jsonl` and lock-protected atomic writes for mutable `groups.json`; either share the thread index lock or use a per-thread groups lock with tmp write, fsync, and replace.
3. **Maintain** denormalized `.artagents/threads/<thread-id>/groups.json` for variant lookup, keepers, and descendants after run writes and selection writes.
4. **Use** `role: variant | other` only, defaulting to `other`.
5. **Implement** `thread keep <run-id>:<n>[,<n>]`, `thread keep <run-id>:none`, and `thread group <run-id> <run-id> ...`.
6. **Emit** `[variants]` only for unresolved variants and silence it after keep or explicit dismiss.
7. **Document** the SD-042 append-only sentence in help and prefix guidance.

4. **Inject** `pipeline.provenance` into `hype.metadata.json` through the orchestrator/wrapper integration path or a small shared helper called from hype orchestration, not by modifying forbidden executor producer files outside SD-031.

### Step 16: Test variants and provenance (`tests/test_threads_variants.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py`, `tests/test_threads_groups_concurrency.py`, `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_threads_brief_private.py`, audit bridge tests)

## Phase 5: Modality Registry and Iteration Prepare

### Step 17: Add modality registry (`artagents/modalities/`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** exactly three renderer modules: `image_grid`, `audio_waveform`, and `generic_card`.
2. **Register** `generic_card` last as the loud fallback.
3. **Add** `python3 -m artagents modalities list` and `python3 -m artagents modalities inspect <renderer>` and route them through `artagents/pipeline.py`.
4. **Dispatch** by artifact `kind`; do not introduce `preview_modes`.

### Step 18: Create `iteration.prepare` executor (`artagents/executors/iteration_prepare/{executor.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Read** its own new `STAGE.md` before invoking during implementation.
2. **Collect** the provenance graph by walking typed `parent_run_ids` backward from a target run, using bridged audit/thread provenance and artifact hashes.
3. **Label** runs as `in_thread` or `pulled_by_ancestry`.
4. **Compute** `data_quality` with the OQ-6 formula and emit `iteration.quality.json`.
5. **Count** candidate runs and enforce `max_iterations` before uncached `builtin.understand` dispatch.
6. **Summarize** through `builtin.understand` with `ThreadPoolExecutor`, `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY` default 4, exponential backoff, and `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_SEQUENTIAL=1` debug fallback.
7. **Cache** summaries under `.artagents/iteration_cache/<run_id>__<summarizer_model_version>.json`; evaluate cap against uncached summarize count after cache lookup.
8. **Use** a documented default cost-per-call constant in code/config for v1 inspect estimates, overrideable by environment or flag.
9. **Emit** draft `iteration.manifest.json` with ordered runs, renderer candidates, quality score, cache hits/misses, cost metadata, and allocation hints.
10. **Order** deterministically by causal depth, selection-event tiebreaks, then run ULID.

### Step 19: Test modalities and prepare (`tests/test_modalities_registry.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_collect.py`, `tests/test_iteration_quality.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_cap.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_score.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify** exactly three renderers, top-level route reachability, fallback order, and inspect output.
2. **Verify** graph collection, audit/thread provenance reconciliation, in-thread/pulled-by-ancestry labels, valid-root handling, unresolved-producer reporting, and quality formula.
3. **Verify** cap refusal before summarize calls, direct executor invocation enforcement, cache hit/miss behavior, cost estimate inputs, and stable ordering.

## Phase 6: Iteration Assemble, Render Adapter, and Orchestrator

### Step 20: Create `iteration.assemble` executor as the render adapter (`artagents/executors/iteration_assemble/{executor.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Read** its own new `STAGE.md` before invoking during implementation.
2. **Consume** prepare outputs; do not re-walk the graph or re-run summarization.
3. **Resolve** renderers by `kind` only, with loud `generic_card` fallback diagnostics and report payload.
4. **Accept** `--direction` as label-only; do not parse natural language into structured instructions.
5. **Support** v1 chaptered mode only and reject or clearly defer parallel/interleaved.
6. **Select** audio bed automatically per SD-028 and never generate music.
7. **Enforce** `data_quality >= 0.6` before any render handoff, with `--force` bypass logged as `forced: true`.
8. **Emit** canonical human-facing outputs: `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html`, and `iteration.quality.json`.
9. **Materialize** render-compatible adapter files in the same `brief_out`: copy or write the timeline to `hype.timeline.json` and create the matching `hype.assets.json` expected by `builtin.render`.
10. **Guarantee** `iteration.timeline.json` and `hype.timeline.json` are equivalent for render purposes; if both are written separately, add a test comparing their parsed timeline content.
11. **Do not call** `builtin.cut` after assemble. Assemble has already produced the required `hype.timeline.json`/`hype.assets.json` pair, so running cut after assemble would risk overwriting the iteration timeline.

### Step 21: Add `builtin.iteration_video` orchestrator with explicit render handoff (`artagents/orchestrators/iteration_video/{orchestrator.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Wire** the v1 chain as `iteration.prepare -> iteration.assemble -> builtin.render -> finalize iteration outputs`.
2. **Call** existing `builtin.render` with `brief_out` pointing at the directory containing `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json` created by assemble.
3. **After render**, copy or rename `{brief_out}/hype.mp4` to `iteration.mp4` and record both paths only if needed, with `iteration.mp4` as the SD-022 output artifact.
4. **Keep** `hype.mp4` as an intermediate compatibility artifact unless the existing render path can safely target `iteration.mp4` directly without changing shared render semantics.
5. **Add** `iteration_video inspect <thread>` as the primary discovery command that does not render and does not dispatch `builtin.understand`.
6. **Print** detected modalities, chosen renderers, quality, cache hit/miss counts, and a single DEF-9 cost estimate line using the documented v1 cost-per-call default/override.
7. **Support** v1 flags: `--renderers`, `--clip-mode`, label-only `--direction`, `--mode chaptered`, `--audio-bed`, and `--max-iterations`.
8. **Produce** all five SD-022 outputs as one variant group: `iteration.mp4`, `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html`, and `iteration.quality.json`.
9. **Honor** `--no-content` in the report; keep hashes, labels, and structural provenance while suppressing plaintext prompt/brief content.
10. **Include** `in-thread`/`pulled-by-ancestry` labels and fallback annotations in the HTML report.

### Step 22: Test assemble, render handoff, and orchestrator (`tests/test_iteration_assemble.py`, `tests/test_quality_floor.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_fallback.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_inspect.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_outputs.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_orchestrator.py`, `tests/test_iteration_render_contract.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify** kind-based renderer resolution, fallback diagnostics, timeline/manifest output, style precedence, audio-bed rule, v1 mode handling, and no `preview_modes` dependency.
2. **Verify** quality floor refusal happens before `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, or render dispatch unless `--force` is used.
3. **Verify** `iteration.assemble` writes `iteration.timeline.json`, render-compatible `hype.timeline.json`, and `hype.assets.json` into the expected `brief_out`.
4. **Verify** `builtin.render` is invoked with existing `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json` names.
5. **Verify** `{brief_out}/hype.mp4` is copied or renamed to `iteration.mp4` and that `iteration.mp4` is the recorded SD-022 video output.
6. **Verify** `builtin.cut` is not invoked after assemble in the normal iteration-video render path.
7. **Verify** inspect performs discovery without render/summarize dispatch and prints the single-number cost block.
8. **Verify** the five-output set, variant group metadata, role defaults, report privacy, ancestry labels, and max-iterations forwarding.

## Execution Order
1. Audit top-level routing, runner callers, audit provenance, and render input/output naming before implementation.
2. Add unconditional dependency and foundational thread state before runner wrappers.
3. Implement failure-safe runner wrapping with try/except/finally and missing-output handling before CLI expansion.
4. Register top-level routes as soon as `thread` and `modalities` CLIs exist.
5. Implement attribution, lifecycle, prefix, and thread CLI before variants.
6. Implement variants with locked `groups.json` writes before provenance and iteration-video consumption.
7. Bridge existing audit provenance before iteration prepare walks ancestry.
8. Add modalities and prepare before assemble.
9. Implement assemble as the adapter that writes both `iteration.*` and render-compatible `hype.*` files.
10. Wire `builtin.iteration_video` to call `builtin.render` only after adapter files exist, then finalize `iteration.mp4` from `hype.mp4`.
11. Add tracked compact dogfood fixture before relying on local ignored `runs/artagents_logo_v3`.
12. Finish with full regression and scope audit.

## Validation Order
1. Baseline CLI smoke checks.
2. Dependency and state-layer tests.
3. Runner tests, especially exception finalization, missing/empty `out`, no-op gates, and nested subprocess inheritance.
4. Top-level route tests for `thread` and `modalities`.
5. Attribution, prefix, lifecycle, reaper, no-content, and backfill tests.
6. Variant tests, especially concurrent `groups.json` and append-only selection semantics.
7. Provenance bridge tests against existing audit data and hype metadata output.
8. Modality registry and prepare tests.
9. Assemble adapter tests for `iteration.timeline.json`, `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and quality-floor refusal before render handoff.
10. Orchestrator render contract tests proving `builtin.render` consumes existing `hype.*` files and `hype.mp4` becomes `iteration.mp4`.
11. Compact dogfood fixture and transcript replay tests.
12. Full pytest suite and diff/scope audit.

        Settled decisions (verify the executor implemented these correctly):
- GATE-SD-001: Proceed with implementation from plan version 3. (All blocking critique categories are clear and weighted score is 0.)
- GATE-SD-002: Do not implement or reference `thread gc`. (SD-036 cuts it; stale-lock remediation must use supported diagnostics/manual guidance.)
- GATE-SD-003: Use `iteration.assemble` as the render adapter. (It must emit canonical `iteration.*` outputs plus render-compatible `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json`; `builtin.render` consumes the `hype.*` files and `hype.mp4` becomes recorded `iteration.mp4`.)
- GATE-SD-004: Bridge thread provenance with the existing audit ledger. (Avoid divergent ancestry sources by using audit parent/assets where available and hash matching as fallback.)
- GATE-SD-005: Use a tracked compact dogfood fixture for CI and ignored `runs/artagents_logo_v3` for local manual dogfood. (This keeps automated verification deterministic without committing generated media.)

        Requirements:
        - Verify each success criterion explicitly.
        - Trust executor evidence by default. Dig deeper only where the git diff, `execution_audit.json`, or vague notes make the claim ambiguous.
        - Each criterion has a `priority` (`must`, `should`, or `info`). Apply these rules:
          - `must` criteria are hard gates. A `must` criterion that fails means `needs_rework`.
          - `should` criteria are quality targets. If the spirit is met but the letter is not, mark `pass` with evidence explaining the gap. Only mark `fail` if the intent was clearly missed. A `should` failure alone does NOT require `needs_rework`.
          - `info` criteria are for human reference. Mark them `waived` with a note — do not evaluate them.
          - If a criterion has `requires` capabilities that are not satisfiable by container workers (e.g., `drive_browser`, `subjective_judgment`), mark it `deferred_human` — NOT `fail` or `waived`. Deferred-human criteria do NOT count toward `needs_rework`.
          - If a criterion (any priority) cannot be verified in this context (e.g., requires manual testing or runtime observation), mark it `waived` with an explanation.
        - Set `review_verdict` to `needs_rework` only when at least one `must` criterion fails or actual implementation work is incomplete. Use `approved` when all `must` criteria pass, even if some `should` criteria are flagged.
        - The decisions listed above were settled at the gate stage. Verify that the executor implemented each settled decision correctly. Flag deviations from these decisions, but do not question the decisions themselves.
        - baseline_test_failures in finalize.json lists tests that were already failing before execution. Do not flag these as rework items unless the executor introduced new failures in those same tests.
        - Cross-reference each task's `files_changed` and `commands_run` against the git diff and any audit findings.
        - Review every `sense_check` explicitly and treat perfunctory acknowledgments as a reason to dig deeper.
        - Follow this JSON shape exactly:
        ```json
        {
          "review_verdict": "approved",
          "criteria": [
            {
              "name": "All existing tests pass",
              "priority": "must",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "Test suite ran green — 42 passed, 0 failed."
            },
            {
              "name": "File under ~300 lines",
              "priority": "should",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "File is 375 lines — above the target but reasonable given the component's responsibilities. Spirit met."
            },
            {
              "name": "Manual smoke tests pass",
              "priority": "info",
              "pass": "waived",
              "evidence": "Cannot be verified in automated review. Noted for manual QA."
            }
          ],
          "issues": [],
          "rework_items": [],
          "summary": "Approved. All must criteria pass. The should criterion on line count is close enough given the component scope.",
          "task_verdicts": [
            {
              "task_id": "T6",
              "reviewer_verdict": "Pass. Claimed handler changes and command evidence match the repo state.",
              "evidence_files": ["megaplan/handlers.py", "megaplan/evaluation.py"]
            }
          ],
          "sense_check_verdicts": [
            {
              "sense_check_id": "SC6",
              "verdict": "Confirmed. The execute blocker only fires when both evidence arrays are empty."
            }
          ]
        }
        ```
        - The parallel-review bookkeeping fields `checks`, `pre_check_flags`, `verified_flag_ids`, and `disputed_flag_ids` are optional for this criteria/task/sense-check review shape. Include them only when they are present in the output template or explicitly requested above.
        - `rework_items` must be an array of structured rework directives. When `review_verdict` is `needs_rework`, populate one entry per issue with:
          - `task_id`: which finalize task this issue relates to
          - `issue`: what is wrong
          - `expected`: what correct behavior looks like
          - `actual`: what was observed
          - `evidence_file` (optional): file path supporting the finding
          - `flag_id`: critique/review flag ID when applicable, otherwise `null`
          - `source`: short machine-readable source tag when applicable, otherwise `null`
        - `issues` must still be populated as a flat one-line-per-item summary derived from `rework_items` (for backward compatibility). When approved, both `issues` and `rework_items` should be empty arrays.
        - When the work needs another execute pass, keep the same shape and change only `review_verdict` to `needs_rework`; make `issues`, `rework_items`, `summary`, and task verdicts specific enough for the executor to act on directly.

        # Implementation Plan: Implement ArtAgents Thread / Variant / Iteration-Video Layer

## Overview
Ship the 10-day sprint in `docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`: Week 1 creates the durable thread/run/variant metadata layer at executor and orchestrator chokepoints, and Week 2 consumes that metadata to produce `builtin.iteration_video` outputs.

The new critique does not change the root cause of the overall plan, but it does identify one wrong implementation assumption in the iteration-video phase: `builtin.render` is not a generic renderer for arbitrary `iteration.timeline.json` names. It currently consumes `{brief_out}/hype.timeline.json` and `{brief_out}/hype.assets.json` and emits `{brief_out}/hype.mp4`. The plan must therefore make `iteration.assemble` a render-adapter stage that writes both human-facing iteration artifacts and render-compatible `hype.*` adapter files before invoking `builtin.render`.

The corrected iteration-video approach is: `iteration.prepare -> iteration.assemble -> builtin.render -> finalize/copy outputs`. `iteration.assemble` emits canonical `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.quality.json`, and `iteration.report.html`, and also materializes byte-equivalent render inputs at `{brief_out}/hype.timeline.json` and `{brief_out}/hype.assets.json`. The orchestrator then calls existing `builtin.render` against those `hype.*` names and copies or renames `{brief_out}/hype.mp4` to `iteration.mp4`. `builtin.cut` is not run after assemble because assemble has already produced the render-ready `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json` pair; this follows the repo rule that render can proceed without cut when both files already exist.

The implementation should bridge into existing systems instead of inventing parallel ones: use `artagents/audit/*` and hype audit parent propagation as provenance inputs, add the new thread block at allowed wrapper/orchestrator integration points rather than by modifying forbidden executor `run.py` files, keep generated dogfood media out of CI by using a tracked compact fixture, and preserve all DEF trims.

## Phase 1: Foundation — CLI Audit, Dependencies, IDs, Schema, Index

### Step 1: Audit current routing, provenance, and render surfaces (`artagents/pipeline.py`, `artagents/audit/`, `artagents/orchestrators/hype/run.py`, `artagents/executors/render/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Inspect** top-level command dispatch in `artagents/pipeline.py` and confirm where new `thread` and `modalities` commands must be registered for `python3 -m artagents`.
2. **Inspect** executor/orchestrator CLI construction to identify `out=""`, `out=None`, dry-run, command-runtime, and nested subprocess call paths before adding wrapper behavior.
3. **Inspect** `artagents/audit/*` and hype parent propagation via `ARTAGENTS_AUDIT_PARENT_IDS`; treat this as an existing provenance source to bridge into thread records and iteration-video ancestry.
4. **Inspect** `builtin.render` inputs and hype render command construction; record the compatibility contract that render consumes `{brief_out}/hype.timeline.json` plus `{brief_out}/hype.assets.json` and emits `{brief_out}/hype.mp4`.
5. **Run** cheap baseline checks: `python3 -m artagents --help`, `python3 -m artagents doctor`, `python3 -m artagents executors list`, `python3 -m artagents orchestrators list`, and `python3 -m artagents elements list`.

### Step 2: Add deterministic dependency plumbing (`requirements.txt`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `xxhash>=3.4` unconditionally to `requirements.txt` because SD-032 requires xxhash.
2. **Add** a dependency test that imports `xxhash` and confirms the requirement is declared.

### Step 3: Add thread state package (`artagents/threads/ids.py`, `artagents/threads/schema.py`, `artagents/threads/index.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Implement** monotonic 26-character Crockford ULIDs for `run_id`, `thread_id`, and `group_id`.
2. **Define** v1 schema constants and validators for `.artagents/threads.json`, `run.json`, `selections.jsonl`, `groups.json`, and hype provenance blocks.
3. **Implement** `.artagents/threads.json` with a sidecar lock file, `fcntl.flock`, 30-second timeout, tmp write, fsync, directory fsync, `os.replace`, and `.bak` rotation.
4. **Resolve** the sprint conflict around stale locks by not implementing `thread gc`; timeout errors should name supported manual remediation and `thread show/list` diagnostics, because SD-036 cuts `thread gc`.
5. **Reject** absolute persisted paths at schema/write boundaries; allow only repo-relative paths or content-addressed references.

### Step 4: Prove foundation behavior (`tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Test** schema creation, ULID format, path validation, lock acquisition, lock timeout text, atomic replacement, `.bak` recovery, and eight-writer concurrency.
2. **Test** the lock-timeout message does not reference `thread gc`.
3. **Test** `xxhash` importability and `requirements.txt` declaration.

## Phase 2: Runner Chokepoints and Failure-Safe Records

### Step 5: Build run record helpers (`artagents/threads/record.py`, `artagents/threads/wrapper.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Implement** `threads.begin(request)` and `threads.finalize(record_id, result_or_error)` around a normalized request shape shared by executors and orchestrators.
2. **Handle** missing/empty output paths before repo-relative/private-path logic. `out=None` or `out=""` should either no-op when appropriate or record a safe empty/unknown output state without crashing.
3. **Capture** the trimmed SD-008 field set, typed `parent_run_ids`, xxhash `inputs_digest`, sha256 output artifact hashes, `brief_content_sha256`, redacted CLI args, private-path sha-only metadata, and trimmed external model provenance.
4. **Bridge** existing audit provenance into thread records: map audit parent IDs/assets into typed causal `parent_run_ids` and input/output artifact provenance where available, then fall back to artifact hash matching.
5. **Do not** write trimmed fields: `host_id`, top-level `chosen_from_groups`, `preview_modes`, external seed/cost/latency, or low/high cost ranges.

### Step 6: Wrap executor and orchestrator runners with exception finalization (`artagents/executors/runner.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/runner.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Extend** `ExecutorRunRequest` and `OrchestratorRunRequest` with `thread`, `variants`, and `from_variant`/`from` semantics.
2. **Wrap** `run_executor()` and `run_orchestrator()` with `try/except/finally` so any exception after `begin` writes `ended_at`, failure status, returncode/error metadata, and then re-raises the original exception.
3. **Preserve** no-op gates: dry run, missing/empty unwritable output where no run artifact can be safely recorded, temp output, `--thread @none`, `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1`, inherited child context, and `upload.youtube` zero-artifact behavior.
4. **Pass** `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID` and thread context into `_run_external_executor`, command-runtime orchestrators, and hype pipeline subprocesses launched outside `_run_external_executor`.
5. **Avoid** duplicate child begin records when a nested subprocess inherits `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID`; nested work should attach to parent context.

### Step 7: Wire run flags and top-level routes (`artagents/executors/cli.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `--thread <id|@new|@none>`, `--variants N`, and `--from <run-id>:<n>` to executor and orchestrator run commands.
2. **Register** new `thread` and `modalities` routes in `artagents/pipeline.py` so `python3 -m artagents thread ...` and `python3 -m artagents modalities ...` work through the package gateway.
3. **Keep** CLI parsing thin; centralize attribution, validation, and no-op decisions in `artagents/threads`.

### Step 8: Test runner integration and failure paths (`tests/test_threads_record.py`, `tests/test_threads_cli_plumbing.py`, `tests/test_threads_redaction.py`, `tests/test_threads_upload_youtube_noop.py`, `tests/test_threads_failure_finalization.py`, `tests/test_threads_missing_out.py`, `tests/test_threads_nested.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify** eligible executor and orchestrator runs write/finalize `run.json` and update `.artagents/threads.json`.
2. **Verify** exceptions after begin finalize records before re-raising.
3. **Verify** missing/empty `out` paths do not crash private-path or repo-relative validation.
4. **Verify** all no-op gates create no thread artifacts and no errors.
5. **Verify** nested command-runtime, hype subprocess, and external executor inheritance via `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID`.
6. **Verify** `upload.youtube` bypass behavior and redaction/private-path behavior.

## Phase 3: Attribution, Prefix, Lifecycle, and Thread CLI

### Step 9: Implement attribution and prefix contracts (`artagents/threads/attribute.py`, `artagents/threads/prefix.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Implement** SD-006 attribution under the index lock: explicit thread, lineage inference from `runs/<R>/` input-like args, active open thread, archived active thread within reopen window, then new thread.
2. **Implement** lazy lifecycle windows: `IDLE_ARCHIVE_WINDOW=7d`, `IDLE_REOPEN_WINDOW=48h`, open to archived to reopen, no daemon.
3. **Implement** SD-018 stdout prefix generation: `[thread]`, `[variants]` when present, optional `Notice:`, blank line, then command output.
4. **Honor** DEF trims: Notice tier only, no `Warn:` brief novelty, no `[thread-health]`, no `[hint]` fan-out.

### Step 10: Add thread commands (`artagents/threads/cli.py`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `python3 -m artagents thread new`, `list`, `show`, `archive`, `reopen`, and `backfill`.
2. **Support** `thread show @active --no-content` with IDs, labels, hashes, and status but no plaintext brief/prompt content.
3. **Do not add** `thread gc`, `split`, `merge`, `attach`, or `detach`.
4. **Implement** `thread backfill` for existing `runs/*/` directories without moving files, clustered by hash graph rather than mtime.

### Step 11: Add in-flight reaper (`artagents/threads/index.py` or `artagents/threads/record.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Run** the reaper lazily once per process on first index read.
2. **Mark** dead in-flight records with `returncode: -1`, `ended_at`, and `status: orphaned` when the owner PID is gone.
3. **Keep** exception finalization as the primary crash path; reaper is only for truly abandoned records.

### Step 12: Test attribution, lifecycle, prefix, and thread CLI (`tests/test_threads_attribute.py`, `tests/test_threads_prefix.py`, `tests/test_threads_lifecycle.py`, `tests/test_threads_reaper.py`, `tests/test_threads_backfill.py`, `tests/test_threads_no_content.py`, `tests/test_threads_cli_routes.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Cover** all attribution branches with frozen time, including lineage precedence.
2. **Cover** prefix stdout placement, order, width/truncation/color gates, Notice triggers, and absence of deferred line classes.
3. **Cover** lifecycle archive/reopen behavior, once-per-process reaper behavior, backfill adoption, hash-graph clustering, no file moves, no-content suppression, and top-level command reachability.

## Phase 4: Variants, Locked Group Indexes, and Provenance

### Step 13: Implement variant state (`artagents/threads/selections.py`, `artagents/threads/groups.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** append-only `.artagents/threads/<thread-id>/selections.jsonl` events with last-write-wins read semantics.
2. **Use** atomic append for `selections.jsonl` and lock-protected atomic writes for mutable `groups.json`; either share the thread index lock or use a per-thread groups lock with tmp write, fsync, and replace.
3. **Maintain** denormalized `.artagents/threads/<thread-id>/groups.json` for variant lookup, keepers, and descendants after run writes and selection writes.
4. **Use** `role: variant | other` only, defaulting to `other`.
5. **Implement** `thread keep <run-id>:<n>[,<n>]`, `thread keep <run-id>:none`, and `thread group <run-id> <run-id> ...`.
6. **Emit** `[variants]` only for unresolved variants and silence it after keep or explicit dismiss.
7. **Document** the SD-042 append-only sentence in help and prefix guidance.

### Step 14: Patch only allowed variant producers (`artagents/executors/generate_image/run.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/logo_ideas/run.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Stamp** generated image siblings as `role: variant` with `group`, `group_index`, and applicable metadata.
2. **Fold** logo candidate metadata (`name`, `rationale`, `prompt`, `generated.*`) into `variant_meta`.
3. **Confirm** no other executor `run.py` is modified for variant opt-in.

### Step 15: Add brief snapshotting and hype provenance bridge (`artagents/threads/wrapper.py`, `artagents/orchestrators/hype/run.py`, audit bridge helper)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Write** `runs/<slug>/brief.copy.txt` for eligible runs and stamp `brief_content_sha256` from the snapshot.
2. **Keep** private brief behavior path-based only: `runs/<slug>/private/` means sha/label-only metadata; do not add a dedicated brief privacy flag.
3. **Bridge** thread provenance with the existing audit ledger rather than creating an unrelated graph. Thread records should consume audit parent IDs/assets when present and keep artifact-hash resolution as a fallback.
4. **Inject** `pipeline.provenance` into `hype.metadata.json` through the orchestrator/wrapper integration path or a small shared helper called from hype orchestration, not by modifying forbidden executor producer files outside SD-031.
5. **Include** `schema_version`, `thread_id`, denormalized `thread_label`, `run_id`, typed `parent_run_ids[]`, `contributing_runs[]`, `starred`, and `agent_version`.

### Step 16: Test variants and provenance (`tests/test_threads_variants.py`, `tests/test_threads_variants_help.py`, `tests/test_threads_groups_concurrency.py`, `tests/test_threads_provenance.py`, `tests/test_threads_brief_private.py`, audit bridge tests)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify** heterogeneous output grouping includes only `role: variant` artifacts and defaults everything else to `other`.
2. **Verify** concurrent keep/group updates do not lose `groups.json` changes.
3. **Verify** selection append history and latest-write keeper reads.
4. **Verify** typed chosen edges in `parent_run_ids`.
5. **Verify** hype provenance uses/reconciles audit data and survives deletion of `.artagents/threads.json` through denormalized fields.
6. **Verify** brief snapshot hashes and private-path sha-only handling.

## Phase 5: Modality Registry and Iteration Prepare

### Step 17: Add modality registry (`artagents/modalities/`, `artagents/pipeline.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** exactly three renderer modules: `image_grid`, `audio_waveform`, and `generic_card`.
2. **Register** `generic_card` last as the loud fallback.
3. **Add** `python3 -m artagents modalities list` and `python3 -m artagents modalities inspect <renderer>` and route them through `artagents/pipeline.py`.
4. **Dispatch** by artifact `kind`; do not introduce `preview_modes`.

### Step 18: Create `iteration.prepare` executor (`artagents/executors/iteration_prepare/{executor.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Read** its own new `STAGE.md` before invoking during implementation.
2. **Collect** the provenance graph by walking typed `parent_run_ids` backward from a target run, using bridged audit/thread provenance and artifact hashes.
3. **Label** runs as `in_thread` or `pulled_by_ancestry`.
4. **Compute** `data_quality` with the OQ-6 formula and emit `iteration.quality.json`.
5. **Count** candidate runs and enforce `max_iterations` before uncached `builtin.understand` dispatch.
6. **Summarize** through `builtin.understand` with `ThreadPoolExecutor`, `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY` default 4, exponential backoff, and `ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_SEQUENTIAL=1` debug fallback.
7. **Cache** summaries under `.artagents/iteration_cache/<run_id>__<summarizer_model_version>.json`; evaluate cap against uncached summarize count after cache lookup.
8. **Use** a documented default cost-per-call constant in code/config for v1 inspect estimates, overrideable by environment or flag.
9. **Emit** draft `iteration.manifest.json` with ordered runs, renderer candidates, quality score, cache hits/misses, cost metadata, and allocation hints.
10. **Order** deterministically by causal depth, selection-event tiebreaks, then run ULID.

### Step 19: Test modalities and prepare (`tests/test_modalities_registry.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_collect.py`, `tests/test_iteration_quality.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_cap.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_cache.py`, `tests/test_iteration_prepare_score.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify** exactly three renderers, top-level route reachability, fallback order, and inspect output.
2. **Verify** graph collection, audit/thread provenance reconciliation, in-thread/pulled-by-ancestry labels, valid-root handling, unresolved-producer reporting, and quality formula.
3. **Verify** cap refusal before summarize calls, direct executor invocation enforcement, cache hit/miss behavior, cost estimate inputs, and stable ordering.

## Phase 6: Iteration Assemble, Render Adapter, and Orchestrator

### Step 20: Create `iteration.assemble` executor as the render adapter (`artagents/executors/iteration_assemble/{executor.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Read** its own new `STAGE.md` before invoking during implementation.
2. **Consume** prepare outputs; do not re-walk the graph or re-run summarization.
3. **Resolve** renderers by `kind` only, with loud `generic_card` fallback diagnostics and report payload.
4. **Accept** `--direction` as label-only; do not parse natural language into structured instructions.
5. **Support** v1 chaptered mode only and reject or clearly defer parallel/interleaved.
6. **Select** audio bed automatically per SD-028 and never generate music.
7. **Enforce** `data_quality >= 0.6` before any render handoff, with `--force` bypass logged as `forced: true`.
8. **Emit** canonical human-facing outputs: `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html`, and `iteration.quality.json`.
9. **Materialize** render-compatible adapter files in the same `brief_out`: copy or write the timeline to `hype.timeline.json` and create the matching `hype.assets.json` expected by `builtin.render`.
10. **Guarantee** `iteration.timeline.json` and `hype.timeline.json` are equivalent for render purposes; if both are written separately, add a test comparing their parsed timeline content.
11. **Do not call** `builtin.cut` after assemble. Assemble has already produced the required `hype.timeline.json`/`hype.assets.json` pair, so running cut after assemble would risk overwriting the iteration timeline.

### Step 21: Add `builtin.iteration_video` orchestrator with explicit render handoff (`artagents/orchestrators/iteration_video/{orchestrator.yaml,STAGE.md,run.py}`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Wire** the v1 chain as `iteration.prepare -> iteration.assemble -> builtin.render -> finalize iteration outputs`.
2. **Call** existing `builtin.render` with `brief_out` pointing at the directory containing `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json` created by assemble.
3. **After render**, copy or rename `{brief_out}/hype.mp4` to `iteration.mp4` and record both paths only if needed, with `iteration.mp4` as the SD-022 output artifact.
4. **Keep** `hype.mp4` as an intermediate compatibility artifact unless the existing render path can safely target `iteration.mp4` directly without changing shared render semantics.
5. **Add** `iteration_video inspect <thread>` as the primary discovery command that does not render and does not dispatch `builtin.understand`.
6. **Print** detected modalities, chosen renderers, quality, cache hit/miss counts, and a single DEF-9 cost estimate line using the documented v1 cost-per-call default/override.
7. **Support** v1 flags: `--renderers`, `--clip-mode`, label-only `--direction`, `--mode chaptered`, `--audio-bed`, and `--max-iterations`.
8. **Produce** all five SD-022 outputs as one variant group: `iteration.mp4`, `iteration.timeline.json`, `iteration.manifest.json`, `iteration.report.html`, and `iteration.quality.json`.
9. **Honor** `--no-content` in the report; keep hashes, labels, and structural provenance while suppressing plaintext prompt/brief content.
10. **Include** `in-thread`/`pulled-by-ancestry` labels and fallback annotations in the HTML report.

### Step 22: Test assemble, render handoff, and orchestrator (`tests/test_iteration_assemble.py`, `tests/test_quality_floor.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_fallback.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_inspect.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_no_content.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_outputs.py`, `tests/test_iteration_video_orchestrator.py`, `tests/test_iteration_render_contract.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify** kind-based renderer resolution, fallback diagnostics, timeline/manifest output, style precedence, audio-bed rule, v1 mode handling, and no `preview_modes` dependency.
2. **Verify** quality floor refusal happens before `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, or render dispatch unless `--force` is used.
3. **Verify** `iteration.assemble` writes `iteration.timeline.json`, render-compatible `hype.timeline.json`, and `hype.assets.json` into the expected `brief_out`.
4. **Verify** `builtin.render` is invoked with existing `hype.timeline.json` and `hype.assets.json` names.
5. **Verify** `{brief_out}/hype.mp4` is copied or renamed to `iteration.mp4` and that `iteration.mp4` is the recorded SD-022 video output.
6. **Verify** `builtin.cut` is not invoked after assemble in the normal iteration-video render path.
7. **Verify** inspect performs discovery without render/summarize dispatch and prints the single-number cost block.
8. **Verify** the five-output set, variant group metadata, role defaults, report privacy, ancestry labels, and max-iterations forwarding.

## Phase 7: Docs, Dogfood, and Final Acceptance

### Step 23: Update docs and agent guidance (`docs/threads.md`, `SKILL.md`, inspect footers)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `docs/threads.md` with model overview, prefix explanation, privacy/redaction, concurrent variant selection, tier firing rules, inspect-before-render, stale-lock remediation without `thread gc`, render handoff notes, and deferred features.
2. **Append** the exact required `SKILL.md` paragraph about `thread show @active`, `[thread]` prefix, and append-only selections.
3. **Add** executor and orchestrator inspect footers naming the active thread and pointing to `python3 -m artagents thread show @active`.
4. **Do not** edit existing per-tool `STAGE.md` files except for the new tools being created.

### Step 24: Add CI-verifiable dogfood fixture and local dogfood path (`tests/fixtures/iteration_video/`, `runs/artagents_logo_v3`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Create** a tracked compact synthetic fixture under `tests/fixtures/iteration_video/` that represents the `artagents_logo_v3` thread shape, variant groups, provenance labels, quality data, render adapter files, and five-output contract without committing generated media.
2. **Use** the compact fixture for CI tests of backfill, inspect, render planning, report generation, fallback injection, no-content mode, render handoff, and five-output metadata.
3. **Reserve** ignored `runs/artagents_logo_v3` for local dogfood: backfill/adopt it, run `thread show @active`, run `iteration_video inspect`, render a 60-second `iteration.mp4`, and capture a local transcript.
4. **Convert** the compact dogfood transcript/fixture into `tests/test_dogfood_failure_modes.py` so CI can verify prefix coverage, active thread correctness, lineage inference, variants nag/silence, pre-render thread inspection, and render handoff.
5. **Keep** the real 60-second video playback checklist as an info-level human acceptance item, not a CI `must` gate.

### Step 25: Final regression and scope audit
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Run** targeted new tests first by area, then `pytest tests/test_doctor_setup.py tests/test_canonical_cli.py`, then `pytest --tb=no -q --no-header`.
2. **Audit** the diff to confirm no executor `run.py` files beyond `generate_image` and `logo_ideas` were modified for variant opt-in.
3. **Audit** persisted schema output to confirm no DEF-trimmed fields slipped into v1.
4. **Audit** `artagents/pipeline.py` for `thread` and `modalities` route registration.
5. **Audit** provenance flow to confirm thread records bridge to existing audit data rather than creating divergent ancestry.
6. **Audit** iteration-video render flow to confirm assemble writes `hype.timeline.json`/`hype.assets.json`, render consumes those names, and `hype.mp4` becomes `iteration.mp4`.
7. **Validate** docs mention SD-036 cuts and DEF-1 through DEF-10 trims.

## Execution Order
1. Audit top-level routing, runner callers, audit provenance, and render input/output naming before implementation.
2. Add unconditional dependency and foundational thread state before runner wrappers.
3. Implement failure-safe runner wrapping with try/except/finally and missing-output handling before CLI expansion.
4. Register top-level routes as soon as `thread` and `modalities` CLIs exist.
5. Implement attribution, lifecycle, prefix, and thread CLI before variants.
6. Implement variants with locked `groups.json` writes before provenance and iteration-video consumption.
7. Bridge existing audit provenance before iteration prepare walks ancestry.
8. Add modalities and prepare before assemble.
9. Implement assemble as the adapter that writes both `iteration.*` and render-compatible `hype.*` files.
10. Wire `builtin.iteration_video` to call `builtin.render` only after adapter files exist, then finalize `iteration.mp4` from `hype.mp4`.
11. Add tracked compact dogfood fixture before relying on local ignored `runs/artagents_logo_v3`.
12. Finish with full regression and scope audit.

## Validation Order
1. Baseline CLI smoke checks.
2. Dependency and state-layer tests.
3. Runner tests, especially exception finalization, missing/empty `out`, no-op gates, and nested subprocess inheritance.
4. Top-level route tests for `thread` and `modalities`.
5. Attribution, prefix, lifecycle, reaper, no-content, and backfill tests.
6. Variant tests, especially concurrent `groups.json` and append-only selection semantics.
7. Provenance bridge tests against existing audit data and hype metadata output.
8. Modality registry and prepare tests.
9. Assemble adapter tests for `iteration.timeline.json`, `hype.timeline.json`, `hype.assets.json`, and quality-floor refusal before render handoff.
10. Orchestrator render contract tests proving `builtin.render` consumes existing `hype.*` files and `hype.mp4` becomes `iteration.mp4`.
11. Compact dogfood fixture and transcript replay tests.
12. Full pytest suite and diff/scope audit.

        - Verify each success criterion explicitly.
        - Trust executor evidence by default. Dig deeper only where the git diff, `execution_audit.json`, or vague notes make the claim ambiguous.
        - Each criterion has a `priority` (`must`, `should`, or `info`). Apply these rules:
          - `must` criteria are hard gates. A `must` criterion that fails means `needs_rework`.
          - `should` criteria are quality targets. If the spirit is met but the letter is not, mark `pass` with evidence explaining the gap. Only mark `fail` if the intent was clearly missed. A `should` failure alone does NOT require `needs_rework`.
          - `info` criteria are for human reference. Mark them `waived` with a note — do not evaluate them.
          - If a criterion has `requires` capabilities that are not satisfiable by container workers (e.g., `drive_browser`, `subjective_judgment`), mark it `deferred_human` — NOT `fail` or `waived`. Deferred-human criteria do NOT count toward `needs_rework`.
          - If a criterion (any priority) cannot be verified in this context (e.g., requires manual testing or runtime observation), mark it `waived` with an explanation.
        - Set `review_verdict` to `needs_rework` only when at least one `must` criterion fails or actual implementation work is incomplete. Use `approved` when all `must` criteria pass, even if some `should` criteria are flagged.
        - The decisions listed above were settled at the gate stage. Verify that the executor implemented each settled decision correctly. Flag deviations from these decisions, but do not question the decisions themselves.

        ```json

          "criteria": [
            {
              "name": "All existing tests pass",
              "priority": "must",
              "pass": "pass",

            },

            },
            {

              "priority": "info",
              "pass": "waived",
              "evidence": "Cannot be verified in automated review. Noted for manual QA."
            }
          ],
          "issues": [],
          "rework_items": [],
          "summary": "Approved. All must criteria pass. The should criterion on line count is close enough given the component scope.",

            }
          ]

        - `rework_items` must be an array of structured rework directives. When `review_verdict` is `needs_rework`, populate one entry per issue with:
          - `task_id`: which finalize task this issue relates to
          - `issue`: what is wrong
          - `expected`: what correct behavior looks like
          - `actual`: what was observed
          - `evidence_file` (optional): file path supporting the finding
          - `flag_id`: critique/review flag ID when applicable, otherwise `null`
          - `source`: short machine-readable source tag when applicable, otherwise `null`
        - `issues` must still be populated as a flat one-line-per-item summary derived from `rework_items` (for backward compatibility). When approved, both `issues` and `rework_items` should be empty arrays.
        - When the work needs another execute pass, keep the same shape and change only `review_verdict` to `needs_rework`; make `issues`, `rework_items`, `summary`, and task verdicts specific enough for the executor to act on directly.
